# Lever comparison

Ranks hold only inside one group (step, data scope, price version, evidence class) and one baseline. Insufficient evidence is never ranked. Unknown is shown as unknown, never 0. `unmeasured` correctness means not checked.

| 1 | ranked | context.reranker_cut.lexical_overlap | rc-v1 | 12.605434118 | 0.02779799747194288828469068966 | [0.0242, 0.0316] | 2000 of 2000 | unmeasured (not checked) | heuristic | 0.027797997 | unknown | unknown | unknown | p50 28.189 ms, p95 35.341 ms | Token sizing, not a quality result: removing a chunk can remove the one the answer needed; answer quality is not measured (FR-CTX-03).; S-6 (within-message dedup, Pipeline C) runs first: exact copies (same h_exact) are collapsed before ranking; their tokens are S-6's, not counted here.; Tokens = removed chunk characters x 0.3384 tokens per character (EDA recon.t0rag.coef_rag, hypothetical reconstruction), from uncached input only.; One result per baseline; recorded and repriced cost are never mixed, and each result states its unit in a 'Unit:' line (ADR-0036, ADR-0038).; A chunk without a score is kept and counted, never treated as 0; the floor keeps at least that many unique chunks per request.; The real bge-reranker-v2-m3 run (cut-first item a) was not done.; Scorer: heuristic word overlap of the normalised user message with each chunk (normalize_safe, word tokens, no stopword list).; Unit: price-file units (premium_gpt_5_5). Fees are not priced, so baseline_cost has no fees key (ADR-0036).; Logged tokens less the removed tokens are repriced with the price table's tiers (tier chosen again); every request at gpt-5.5's price: an estimate only, the model is never called (ADR-0026).; Headline chosen on the tune period (Mar-Jul 2026): top_k none, threshold q50 (0.2000), floor 3; most tokens saved with score mass kept >= 0.9 (tune 0.905); rule met: True. Reported on Aug-Oct 2026.; Slices all and llm_only; workflow rows have no cost. Tokens cover every eligible request; cost only those with a known cost (n_eligible_cost_known).; Cached tokens: the saving uses characters, not a_bill_inputTokens; the repriced baselines add cached tokens back for the five models (Review Focus 3).; Removed tokens are priced as uncached input (chunks assumed after the cached prefix); the saving is capped at input minus cached tokens.; Lexical: seeded sample of 2,000 eligible report-period requests; slices all and llm_only are that sample, so the cost share is of RAG requests only (observed: of all LLM requests).; The working tree had uncommitted changes when this ran.; Overhead: offline per-request estimate on a local machine; not a load test (Q-14). apply on one-row frames, 1000 seeded report-period LLM rows, 1 warm-up; p50 28.1893 ms, p95 35.3411 ms, max 112.5649 ms; 2026-11-02, AMD Ryzen 7 5800H with Radeon Graphics, CPython 3.13.9.; Overhead caveat: the headline point; chunks (and text) of the sampled rows are loaded before timing, so reading data is not timed; scoring and the cut are. Not a reranker model's latency. |  |

Fees never change (ADR-0038).; as_logged saving = the cut's tokens x the logged model's implied_input_per_M / 1e6 (reports/full_eda/tables/implied_unit_rates.csv), off the recorded token block after S-6's share.; A model without an implied rate makes the saving unknown: the row leaves both sides (n_saving_unknown), never zero.; Headline chosen on the tune period (Mar-Jul 2026): top_k none, threshold q50 (0.2000), floor 3; most tokens saved with score mass kept >= 0.9 (tune 0.905); rule met: True. Reported on Aug-Oct 2026.; Slices all and llm_only; workflow rows have no cost. Tokens cover every eligible request; cost only those with a known cost (n_eligible_cost_known).; Cached tokens: the saving uses characters, not a_bill_inputTokens; the repriced baselines add cached tokens back for the five models (Review Focus 3).; Removed tokens are priced as uncached input (chunks assumed after the cached prefix); the saving is capped at input minus cached tokens.; Lexical: seeded sample of 2,000 eligible report-period requests; slices all and llm_only are that sample, so the cost share is of RAG requests only (observed: of all LLM requests).; The working tree had uncommitted changes when this ran.; Overhead: offline per-request estimate on a local machine; not a load test (Q-14). apply on one-row frames, 1000 seeded report-period LLM rows, 1 warm-up; p50 28.1893 ms, p95 35.3411 ms, max 112.5649 ms; 2026-11-02, AMD Ryzen 7 5800H with Radeon Graphics, CPython 3.13.9.; Overhead caveat: the headline point; chunks (and text) of the sampled rows are loaded before timing, so reading data is not timed; scoring and the cut are. Not a reranker model's latency. |  |

Reported on Aug-Oct 2026.; Slices all and llm_only; workflow rows have no cost. Tokens cover every eligible request; cost only those with a known cost (n_eligible_cost_known).; Cached tokens: the saving uses characters, not a_bill_inputTokens; the repriced baselines add cached tokens back for the five models (Review Focus 3).; Removed tokens are priced as uncached input (chunks assumed after the cached prefix); the saving is capped at input minus cached tokens.; The working tree had uncommitted changes when this ran.; Missing slices (language, over 8,000 tokens, tenant ranks, traffic-weighted) and the session-clustered interval were added from the same frames (sprint final Stage 1, analysis.lever_enrich).; Added slices count LLM requests with a known cost; the traffic-weighted share weights each bot's saving share by its LLM requests.; Overhead: offline per-request estimate on a local machine; not a load test (Q-14). apply on one-row frames, 1000 seeded report-period LLM rows, 1 warm-up; p50 38.1795 ms, p95 52.8225 ms, max 112.4818 ms; 2026-11-03, Intel(R) Core(TM) i5-1038NG7 CPU @ 2.00GHz, CPython 3.13.5.; Overhead caveat: the headline point; chunks (and text) of the sampled rows are loaded before timing, so reading data is not timed; scoring and the cut are. | 1 | ranked | context.reranker_cut.lexical_overlap | rc-v1 | 0.736441128 | 0.02543019352328326423968842280 | [0.02222, 0.02894] | 2000 of 2000 | unmeasured (not checked) | heuristic | 0.025430194 | unknown | unknown | unknown | p50 66.295 ms, p95 84.878 ms | Token sizing, not a quality result: removing a chunk can remove the one the answer needed; answer quality is not measured (FR-CTX-03).; S-6 (within-message dedup, Pipeline C) runs first: exact copies (same h_exact) are collapsed before ranking; their tokens are S-6's, not counted here.; Tokens = removed chunk characters x 0.3384 tokens per character (EDA recon.t0rag.coef_rag, hypothetical reconstruction), from uncached input only.; One result per baseline; recorded and repriced cost are never mixed, and each result states its unit in a 'Unit:' line (ADR-0036, ADR-0038).; A chunk without a score is kept and counted, never treated as 0; the floor keeps at least that many unique chunks per request.; The real bge-reranker-v2-m3 run (cut-first item a) was not done.; Scorer: heuristic word overlap of the normalised user message with each chunk (normalize_safe, word tokens, no stopword list).; Unit: price-file units (todays_models). Fees are not priced, so baseline_cost has no fees key (ADR-0036).; Logged tokens less the removed tokens are repriced with the price table's tiers (tier chosen again); every request at its logged model's price (the [baselines] price table, replay prices of ADR-0041); a logged model without a price leaves the row out (n_eligible_cost_known), never zero.; Headline chosen on the tune period (Mar-Jul 2026): top_k none, threshold q50 (0.2000), floor 3; most tokens saved with score mass kept >= 0.9 (tune 0.905); rule met: True. Reported on Aug-Oct 2026.; Slices all and llm_only; workflow rows have no cost. Tokens cover every eligible request; cost only those with a known cost (n_eligible_cost_known).; Cached tokens: the saving uses characters, not a_bill_inputTokens; the repriced baselines add cached tokens back for the five models (Review Focus 3).; Removed tokens are priced as uncached input (chunks assumed after the cached prefix); the saving is capped at input minus cached tokens.; Lexical: seeded sample of 2,000 eligible report-period requests; slices all and llm_only are that sample, so the cost share is of RAG requests only (observed: of all LLM requests).; The working tree had uncommitted changes when this ran.; Overhead: offline per-request estimate on a local machine; not a load test (Q-14). apply on one-row frames, 1000 seeded report-period LLM rows, 1 warm-up; p50 66.2951 ms, p95 84.8784 ms, max 174.7597 ms; 2026-11-02, AMD Ryzen 7 5800H with Radeon Graphics, CPython 3.13.9.; Overhead caveat: the headline point; chunks (and text) of the sampled rows are loaded before timing, so reading data is not timed; scoring and the cut are. Not a reranker model's latency. |  |

| 1 | ranked | context.reranker_cut.lexical_overlap | rc-v1 | 0.126036492 | 0.02628658945602711674906286754 | [0.02286, 0.02994] | 2000 of 2000 | unmeasured (not checked) | heuristic | 0.026286589 | unknown | unknown | unknown | p50 28.189 ms, p95 35.341 ms | Token sizing, not a quality result: removing a chunk can remove the one the answer needed; answer quality is not measured (FR-CTX-03).; S-6 (within-message dedup, Pipeline C) runs first: exact copies (same h_exact) are collapsed before ranking; their tokens are S-6's, not counted here.; Tokens = removed chunk characters x 0.3384 tokens per character (EDA recon.t0rag.coef_rag, hypothetical reconstruction), from uncached input only.; One result per baseline; recorded and repriced cost are never mixed, and each result states its unit in a 'Unit:' line (ADR-0036, ADR-0038).; A chunk without a score is kept and counted, never treated as 0; the floor keeps at least that many unique chunks per request.; The real bge-reranker-v2-m3 run (cut-first item a) was not done.; Scorer: heuristic word overlap of the normalised user message with each chunk (normalize_safe, word tokens, no stopword list).; Unit: price-file units (best_single_cheap). Fees are not priced, so baseline_cost has no fees key (ADR-0036).; Logged tokens less the removed tokens are repriced with the price table's tiers (tier chosen again); every request at gpt-5-nano, the cheapest callable model over the report period (cost only; capability and quality not checked).; Headline chosen on the tune period (Mar-Jul 2026): top_k none, threshold q50 (0.2000), floor 3; most tokens saved with score mass kept >= 0.9 (tune 0.905); rule met: True. Reported on Aug-Oct 2026.; Slices all and llm_only; workflow rows have no cost. Tokens cover every eligible request; cost only those with a known cost (n_eligible_cost_known).; Cached tokens: the saving uses characters, not a_bill_inputTokens; the repriced baselines add cached tokens back for the five models (Review Focus 3).; Removed tokens are priced as uncached input (chunks assumed after the cached prefix); the saving is capped at input minus cached tokens.; Lexical: seeded sample of 2,000 eligible report-period requests; slices all and llm_only are that sample, so the cost share is of RAG requests only (observed: of all LLM requests).; The working tree had uncommitted changes when this ran.; Overhead: offline per-request estimate on a local machine; not a load test (Q-14). apply on one-row frames, 1000 seeded report-period LLM rows, 1 warm-up; p50 28.1893 ms, p95 35.3411 ms, max 112.5649 ms; 2026-11-02, AMD Ryzen 7 5800H with Radeon Graphics, CPython 3.13.9.; Overhead caveat: the headline point; chunks (and text) of the sampled rows are loaded before timing, so reading data is not timed; scoring and the cut are. Not a reranker model's latency. |  |
